new_lines\\\\\\\\n            offset += (j2 - j1) - (i2 - i1)\\\\\\\\n        elif tag == 'insert':\\\\\\\\n            new_lines = fix_lines[j1:j2]\\\\\\\\n            result_lines[adj_i1:adj_i1] = new_lines\\\\\\\\n            offset += (j2 - j1)\\\\\\\\n        elif tag == 'delete':\\\\\\\\n            del result_lines[adj_i1:adj_i2]\\\\\\\\n            offset -= (i2 - i1)\\\\\\\\n
